Abstract: When two neutron stars collide, they eject material containing heavy nuclei formed by the rapid neutron capture process ($r$-process). As these nuclei decay, they power a bright optical/near-infrared transient known as a kilonova (KN). Modeling KN emission is a complex problem involving atomic opacities, radiation transport, and heating powered by the thermalization of radioactive decay products like $γ$-rays, $α$-particles, and $β$-particles. For heating by $γ$-rays, many KN modeling codes do full radiation transport calculations. However, heating by $α$- and $β$-particles relies on simplified descriptions of collisions and transport, and remains an important source of uncertainty in KN models. In this paper, we study the thermalization and transport of $β$-particles. To study thermalization, we use evaluated atomic physics data to estimate per-species contributions to energy deposition, scattering, and electron impact ionization, which we make available online. To include non-local effects, we develop a fully relativistic framework for charged particle transport in a spherically symmetric, homologously expanding ejecta, considering two limiting magnetic-field geometries. Non-local energy deposition and escape reduce thermalization efficiency, especially in the innermost and outermost ejecta, lowering the ejecta temperature and ionization state compared to local deposition models. Coulomb scattering partially offsets these effects by trapping particles at intermediate times. Ionization by secondary electrons significantly enhances the overall ionization rate. We provide analytic prescriptions for the spatially dependent thermalization efficiency for use in future light-curve calculations. Our results demonstrate that evaluated atomic data and charged-particle transport should be incorporated into the next generation of KN models.

</details>

### [87] [Demographic senescence as multi-level selection in miniature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00262)
*Ananda Shikhara Bhat,Hanna Kokko*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文通过构建两层Moran过程模型，证明生物衰老产生的死亡模式可以理解为多层级选择（multi-level selection）的产物，揭示了衰老与多层级选择之间的形式等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面上看，多层级选择与衰老（senescence）似乎没有共同点。作者试图证明衰老产生的死亡模式可以被多层级选择的理论框架所解释，建立两者之间的形式联系。

Method: 作者构建了一个两层Moran过程模型，利用其尺度极限（scaling limits）来展示：一个用于模拟群体结构化种群中多层级选择的简单数学框架，同样可以模拟衰老生物体中的损伤累积模式和由此产生的死亡率曲线。

Result: 衰老个体在其生理子系统中累积损伤，"损伤滋生损伤"；损伤越多的个体越容易死亡，因此高损伤个体在较晚年龄类别中被系统性低代表。这种模式与多层级选择中"群体内叛逃者扩散→叛逃者多的群体在群体间竞争中受损→群体选择导致此类群体系统性低代表"的模式完全类似。

Conclusion: 复杂整合生物体中出现的衰老模式，在形式等价于一个代内多层级选择过程：个体内子系统扮演粒子角色，有机体扮演集体角色，选择性消失扮演群体选择角色。

Abstract: Multi-level selection and senescence do not at first sight have much in common. Here, we demonstrate that the emergent mortality patterns generated by demographic senescence can be understood as the product of multi-level selection. We formulate a two-level Moran type process and use its scaling limits to illustrate that a simple mathematical framework that models multi-level selection in group-structured populations also models damage accumulation patterns and resultant mortality curves in ageing organisms. To verbally make the connection, observe that defectors spread within a group consisting of cooperators and defectors; when groups compete against each other, defector-rich groups suffer, and between-group selection causes such groups to be systematically under-represented. Exactly analogously, senescing individuals accumulate damage to physiological sub-systems, and `damage begets damage'; individuals who are more damaged are more likely to die, hence damage-rich individuals are systematically under-represented in later age classes. Thus, emergent senescence patterns in complex, integrated organisms are formally equivalent to the patterns generated by a within-generation multi-level selection process in which intra-organismal sub-systems play the role of particles, organisms play the role of collectives, and selective disappearance plays the role of group selection.

</details>

Abstract: This study presents a comprehensive analysis of bird diversity across Sri Lanka by integrating spatial, temporal, and environmental data. Bird observation records were combined with environmental variables, including weather conditions, air pollution, the Normalized Difference Vegetation Index (NDVI), land cover, elevation, and Artificial Light At Night (ALAN), and rigorously preprocessed to ensure data quality. Spatial analyses were conducted on multiple grid scales (2 km, 5 km, 10 km) to evaluate patterns in species richness while minimizing sampling bias through spatial thinning. Temporal trends were assessed using effort-corrected metrics including rarefied richness and occupancy rates to account for variations in observation effort over time. Environmental drivers of bird diversity were examined using multivariate statistical models, including Poisson Generalized Linear Models (GLMs) and correlation analyses, to identify key associations between ecological factors and species richness. Additionally, community structure, dominance patterns, and beta diversity were analyzed to understand variations in species composition across regions and time. The study found that land-cover type is a stronger predictor of bird diversity than individual continuous variables such as NDVI or temperature alone. Urbanization, measured by ALAN, exhibits nuanced scale-dependent effects, supporting high abundances of a few generalist species while reducing overall richness. The findings provide actionable insights into the patterns and drivers of avian diversity in Sri Lanka, offering a scalable and reproducible framework for biodiversity research and conservation planning.

Abstract: This work addresses an optimal control problem for a SI epidemic model incorporating heterogeneities in resistance and viral load at the population level. Unlike traditional optimal control problems in homogeneous epidemic models, the present approach focuses on an optimal control problem that accounts for population heterogeneity, offering insights from a microscale perspective. The contribution aims to minimize the final size of the infection within a finite time horizon by developing a pharmaceutical strategy, under a supply constraint that translates into an integral equality constraint in the control function. By applying the Pontryagin Minimum Principle, a characterization of an optimal control is provided.

*Yunbei Pan,Tom Chou*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文研究了多物种出生-死亡过程中，种群调控机制（抑制复制、提高死亡率或两者兼有）如何影响宏观随机动力学。通过引入可调调控分配参数α，将d物种出生-死亡过程投影到(d-1)维Moran过程，发现调控机制依赖的扩散张量。对两物种情况推导了固定时间和固定概率，证明不同调控机制即使在完全确定性中性条件下也会随机偏向更依赖出生调控的物种。定义了α依赖的有效种群大小N_e(α)，并通过微扰理论计算了近中性或调控机制异质性种群的谱隙，将其解释为多样性丧失时间尺度。

Method: 引入可调调控分配参数α_i，将d物种出生-死亡过程投影到(d-1)维Moran过程；对两物种情况推导精确固定时间和固定概率；定义α依赖的有效种群大小N_e(α)；使用微扰理论计算近中性或调控机制异质性种群的谱隙。

Abstract: In multispecies birth-death processes, how population regulation -- through suppressed replication, elevated mortality, or both -- affects macroscopic stochastic dynamics has escaped detailed analysis. Here, we show that the distribution of regulation mechanisms can be invisible in deterministic or mean-field dynamics but play a significant role in the diffusive evolution of population frequencies. By introducing a tunable regulation partitioning parameter $α_i$ and projecting a $d$-species birth-death process onto a $(d{-}1)$-dimensional Moran process, we find a regulation-mechanism-dependent diffusion tensor. For the simple two-species case, we derive exact fixation times and probabilities to show how different regulation mechanisms stochastically favors a more birth-regulated species, even under complete deterministic neutrality. Our model also allows us to define an $α$-dependent effective population size $N_{\rm e}(α)$ among neutral species, generalizing its classical interpretation. For near-neutral populations or populations that are heterogeneous in their regulation mechanism, we used perturbation theory to calculate the spectral gap, identifying it with a diversity loss timescale which can also be interpreted as setting an effective population size. Our results are particularly applicable to interacting subpopulations of T cells ("clones") which are near-neutral, are regulated through proliferation and apoptosis, and lose diversity with time.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解人群既往流行产生的群体免疫如何基于抗原相似性影响新变异株的流行规模与动力学模式，解释为何一次波动的成功并不保证未来更大规模的流行。

Method: 构建递归SIR模型，纳入序贯出现的抗原相关变异株，假定基于状态的群体免疫机制，推导易感人群对后续变异株的递归映射关系。

Result: 稳定等规模复发流行在广泛参数范围内成立；强传播与有限抗原逃逸可导致失稳（周期2、更复杂周期甚至混沌）；流行规模在中等R₀时最大，高传播性增强交叉免疫反而降低未来易感性。

Conclusion: 免疫历史决定了复发流行的动力学特征，成功的一波并不意味着未来更大规模流行，高传播性可能因交叉免疫而自我限制。

Abstract: Population immunity carried over from past epidemics of an antigenically variable pathogen influences the epidemic of new variants based on their antigenic similarity to the previous ones. We develop a recurrent SIR model where a population faces sequential, antigenically related variants. The model yields a recurrence map for the population susceptibility to successive variants under the assumption of status-based population immunity. The model reveals that stable, equal-sized recurrent epidemics occur across broad parameter ranges, but can be destabilized when transmission is strong and antigenic escape is limited, leading to period-2 or more, or even more complex epidemic dynamics. Epidemic size is maximized at an intermediate basic reproduction number: higher transmissibility boosts immediate infection but also enhances cross-immunity, reducing future susceptibility of the population. Our results clarify how immune history shapes recurrent epidemics and why success in one wave does not ensure larger future epidemics.

</details>


### [92] [Approximating Peak Prevalence in Multistage SIR Epidemics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01014)

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文研究了多阶段SI(k)R流行病模型中加权感染阶段峰值与实际患病率峰值之间的关系，揭示了不同缩放尺度下的渐近等价性，为"因子二近似"提供了理论基础，并提出了提高近似精度的修正方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多阶段SIR模型中，患病率峰值（peak prevalence）的解析求解比经典指数分布感染期模型困难得多，而加权感染阶段聚合量常被用作患病率的替代指标，但两者间的关系尚不明确，特别是在不同阶段缩放尺度下的差异需要理论澄清。

Method: 通过分析确定性SI(k)R流行病模型中患病率峰值与加权阶段泛函最大值之间的关系，分别在朴素缩放（固定进展率）和Erlang缩放（保持平均感染期不变）两种尺度下进行渐近分析，推导误差界并开发基于曲率和基于参数的修正方法。

Result: 在朴素缩放下，加权峰值与患病率峰值渐近等价，但因子二近似失效；在Erlang缩放下，多阶段模型收敛到延迟形式，患病率和加权阶段泛函分别成为发病率的无加权和三角加权移动平均，为因子二近似提供了理论基础，并识别了其准确适用的参数区间。

Conclusion: 揭示了加权阶段峰值可作为患病率峰值可靠代理的条件，并提供了在标准近似精度不足时进行修正的定量方法，数值实验验证了修正方法在广泛流行病参数范围内的有效性。

Abstract: Estimating peak prevalence is a central problem in epidemic modeling because it determines the period of greatest infectious burden and is closely linked to health-care demand. In multistage SIR models, however, peak prevalence is generally less tractable than in the classical model with exponentially distributed infectious periods. Motivated by the use of weighted infectious-stage aggregates as surrogates for prevalence, we investigate the relationship between the prevalence peak and the maximum of a weighted stage functional in deterministic SI$(k)$R epidemic models. We show that this relationship depends critically on how the stage-progression rate is scaled as the number of infectious stages increases. Under naive scaling, in which the progression rate remains fixed, the weighted peak is asymptotically equivalent to the prevalence peak and the commonly used factor-two approximation fails. Under Erlang scaling, which preserves the mean infectious period, the multistage model converges to a delay formulation in which prevalence and the weighted stage functional become unweighted and triangularly weighted moving averages of incidence.
  This limiting representation provides a theoretical basis for the factor-two approximation and identifies the regimes in which it is accurate. It also explains why this approximation deteriorates as epidemic waves become more sharply peaked. We derive analytical error bounds and develop curvature-based and parameter-based corrections that substantially improve accuracy. Numerical studies confirm these improvements across a broad range of epidemiological parameters. Overall, the results show when weighted-stage peaks can be used reliably as proxies for peak prevalence and how the resulting estimates can be refined when the standard approximation loses accuracy.

</details>

### [93] [Central isogenies and conjugacy classes in reductive groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00088)
*Sean Cotner*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文将Steinberg关于半单元素中心化子分量群的描述推广到单位元情形，解释了当通用覆盖非平展时单位元中心化子非既约的现象，并应用于计算L-参数模空间特殊纤维的通用重数、万有形变环，以及证明$\mathrm{PGL}_p$在特征$p$下不存在Springer同构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Steinberg描述了连通半单代数群$G$中半单元素中心化子的分量群与$G$的基本群之间的关联，但该理论未覆盖单位元情形，而单位元中心化子可能在通用覆盖非平展时出现非既约现象，需要推广该描述以解释这一事实。

Method: 通过推广Steinberg关于半单元素中心化子分量群的描述，将其拓展到单位元情形，建立单位元中心化子非既约性与通用覆盖非平展性之间的理论联系。

Result: 成功推广了Steinberg的描述，解释了单位元中心化子非既约的原因；计算了L-参数模空间特殊纤维中的通用重数和万有形变环；证明了$\mathrm{PGL}_p$在特征$p$下不存在Springer同构。

Conclusion: 本文的推广不仅统一了半单元素与单位元中心化子的理论描述，还给出了具体应用，展示了该推广在模空间和代数群结构研究中的价值。

Abstract: Steinberg described the group of components of the centralizer of a semisimple element of a connected semisimple algebraic group $G$ as a subgroup of the fundamental group of $G$. We show that this description can be generalized to explain the fact that centralizers of unipotent elements can fail to be reduced when the universal cover of $G$ is not étale. As applications, we compute generic multiplicities in the special fibers of moduli spaces of L-parameters and universal deformation rings, and we show there is no Springer isomorphism for $\mathrm{PGL}_p$ in characteristic $p$.

</details>


### [94] [Fukaya categories of Coulomb branches as unique deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00146)
*Jasper van de Kreeke*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文通过相对Fukaya范畴方法研究水平Hilbert方案的辛几何，先移除物质除子计算补集的Fukaya范畴，再求解形变问题，利用Z^2-分化和NilHecke代数恢复Aganagic等人的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Coulomb分支的辛几何结构复杂，直接确定其Fukaya范畴尤为困难。相对Fukaya范畴提供了一种迂回策略：先计算除子补集的Fukaya范畴，再求解形变问题，从而规避直接计算的难点。

Method: 针对水平Hilbert方案这一特例，首先移除物质除子，借助额外的Z^2-分化缩小可能的形变空间。利用已有的除子补集Fukaya范畴描述，使用特定的生成Lagrangian子流形，并将其自同态代数与NilHecke代数建立等同关系，核心步骤是求解形变问题。

Result: 成功求解了形变问题，恢复了Aganagic等人先前得到的结果。

Conclusion: 相对Fukaya范畴方法在水平Hilbert方案上是有效的，通过先计算除子补集再处理形变的策略能够成功恢复已知结果，验证了这一方法在Coulomb分支辛几何研究中的实用性。

Abstract: The symplectic geometry of Coulomb branches is complicated and it is particularly difficult to determine their Fukaya categories. Relative Fukaya categories present an approach to circumvent these difficulties by first computing the Fukaya category of the complement of a divisor and then solving a deformation problem. In this paper, we apply this approach to the specific case of horizontal Hilbert schemes by removing their matter divisor and narrowing down the set of possible deformations through an additional $ \mathbb{Z}^2 $-grading. We utilize an existing description of the Fukaya category after removal of the matter divisor, in particular we use a specific generating Lagrangian and the identification between its endomorphism algebra and the NilHecke algebra. The core of this paper consists of solving the deformation problem, after which we recover the result of Aganagic et al.

</details>

Method: 利用Lusztig的修正量子群及其典范基理论作为主要构造工具，发展基概形上的仿射代数幺半群理论体系，并建立分类定理。

Result: 得到了基概形上分裂约化群为单群的仿射代数幺半群的分类定理，以及轨道闭包的组合描述与正规性、广义Steinberg定理、Vinberg幺半群的有限型整模型等应用结果。

Conclusion: 成功将Vinberg和Rittatore的域上分类理论推广到一般基概形，并获得了若干重要应用，展示了修正量子群在几何表示论中的新应用。

Abstract: We develop a theory of affine algebraic monoids over general base schemes whose unit groups are split reductive groups. Our main result is a classification theorem for such objects, generalizing works of Vinberg and Rittatore over a field. As applications, we obtain combinatorial descriptions and normality properties of orbit closures, prove a Steinberg-type theorem on adjoint quotients of reductive monoids over general base schemes, and construct finite type integral models of the Vinberg monoids. A main tool in our construction is Lusztig's theory of modified quantum groups and their canonical bases.

</details>

*Jonathan Gruber*

Method: 通过中心化子构造在双仿射Hecke代数中定义子代数B，将其视为仿射Fomin-Stanley子代数的v-形变；在An、B2和G2型中，利用仿射Weyl群的结构，证明B_aff存在由有限Weyl群在仿射Weyl群中的陪集索引的典范基。

Result: 在An、B2和G2型中，证明了子代数B_aff具有由仿射Weyl群中有限Weyl群的陪集索引的典范基；提出了该典范基满足正性性质的猜想；展示了该基可用于研究仿射Hecke代数的中心。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了仿射Hecke代数子代数与仿射Weyl群陪集之间的深刻联系，为研究仿射Hecke代数的中心提供了新工具，并提出了有待证明的正性猜想。

Abstract: We introduce and study a subalgebra $\mathcal{B}$ of the affine Hecke algebra, which arises from a centralizer construction in the double affine Hecke algebra, and which may be regarded as a $v$-deformation of the affine Fomin-Stanley subalgebra introduced by Lam as a combinatorial model for the affine Grassmannian homology ring. In types $\mathsf{A}_n$ and $\mathsf{B}_2$ and $\mathsf{G}_2$, we show that $\mathcal{B}_\mathrm{aff}$ admits a canonical basis indexed by the cosets of the finite Weyl group in the affine Weyl group. We also discuss conjectural positivity properties of the canonical basis and explain how it can be used to study the center of the affine Hecke algebra.

</details>


### [97] [Almost multiplicity-one property of spherical varieties over finite fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00496)
*Fulin Chen,Fang Shi,Shaobin Tan*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限域上连通约化群G的连通代数子群H，使得G/H是G-球面簇。针对该配对(G,H)提出了几乎重数一性质，并建立了基于B-稳定子的判别准则，证明了该性质与特征0下的强 tempered 条件类似。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 针对球面簇G/H提出几乎重数一性质，并通过分析B-稳定子建立判别准则。

Result: 建立了几乎重数一性质的判别准则，并指出该性质与特征0下的强 tempered 条件类似。

Conclusion: 在有限域上球面簇的表示理论中，几乎重数一性质与特征0的强 tempered 条件具有类比性。

Abstract: Let $H$ be a connected algebraic subgroup of a connected reductive group $G$ over a finite field $\mathbb F_q$ such that $G/H$ is a $G$-spherical variety, i.e., $G/H$ has an open dense $B$-orbit for each Borel subgroup $B$ of $G$. We formulate, for the pair $(G,H)$, an almost multiplicity-one property. Then we establish a criterion for this property in terms of the $B$-stabilizers on $G/H$. In particular, we will see that this property is analogous to the strongly tempered condition in characteristic $0$.

</details>

Main category: math.RT

Method: 通过理论推导和性质验证的方式，系统证明spetses上单幺特征的多个关键性质。

Result: 成功证明了spetses的单幺特征满足所有Harish-Chandra理论、所有整数d的Ennola d-对偶性、Alvis-Curtis对偶性以及与相对Hecke代数Rouquier块的相容性。

Conclusion: 该结果验证了spetses理论框架的合理性，为复反射群与有限约化群之间更深层的联系提供了理论支持。

Abstract: It has been shown that the theory of unipotent characters of finite reductive groups admits a generalisation to objects whose Weyl group is a spetsial complex reflection group, called spetses. In this paper we prove several natural properties satisfied by the unipotent characters of spetses, in particular the validity of all Harish-Chandra theories as well as the existence of Ennola $d$-alities for all integers $d$, Alvis--Curtis duality, and compatibility with Rouquier blocks of relative Hecke algebras.

</details>

<details>

Method: 通过限制正特征下所考虑的张量阿贝尔范畴类别，重新建立严格多项式函子范畴的泛性质。

Result: 在特征零时性质成立，正特征下失败但可通过限制范畴类别来修复；新泛性质能恢复若干已知构造，并揭示严格多项式函子的Ext-代数对其他上同调计算的作用。

Conclusion: 严格多项式函子范畴的泛性质依赖于特征条件，正特征下需调整范畴类别才能保持；该结果具有广泛的应用价值，可统一并连接多个领域中的上同调计算。

Abstract: In characteristic zero, the category of strict polynomial functors is well-known to be the tensor abelian category freely generated by one object. We show that this property fails in positive characteristic, but that it can be repaired by restricting the class of tensor abelian categories considered. The new universal property recovers several known constructions and shows that Ext-algebras of strict polynomial functors act on cohomological computations in many other contexts.

</details>

Abstract: We provide a recurrence for computing the terms of the OEIS sequence A393920, introduced in \cite{KS}. We also describe a surprising connection between A393920 and the Fibonacci sequence A000045, obtain non-trivial lower and upper exponential bounds for its growth, and investigate connections with partial orders, Catalan numbers, and convex topologies on finite chains. Abstract: We study quiver Hecke algebras of untwisted affine type $A$ with an arbitrary choice of parameters and establish a duality with the Iwahori-Hecke algebra of the symmetric group. The parameter $t$ of the Iwahori-Hecke algebra is explicitly determined by the parameters defining the quiver Hecke algebra. This duality provides a deformation of the imaginary Schur-Weyl duality introduced by Kleshchev and Muth. Furthermore, we prove that the characters of simple modules in the imaginary strata are computed in terms of the dual canonical basis and Kazhdan-Lusztig polynomials, and the characters of standard modules coincide with the PBW vectors of the corresponding quantum group under certain assumptions.

As in type $A$, we apply this duality to the computation of characters of simple and standard modules.

### [102] [Large silting mutation in extriangulated categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01058)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Silting mutation in triangulated categories, both at the level of objects and of subcategories, was introduced in arXiv:1009.3370, and later generalized to extriangulated categories in arXiv:2303.08125. It simultaneously encompasses the mutation theories of cluster-tilting objects in cluster theory and of compact 2-term silting complexes and support $τ$-tilting modules in $τ$-tilting theory. In this article, we develop an infinite-dimensional analog of silting mutation in extriangulated categories with set-indexed (co)products, which we then apply to obtain a theory of mutation for $n$-cosilting complexes over an arbitrary ring, as well as for infinite-dimensional $n$-(co)tilting modules over a ring of finite global dimension. The former theory is also shown to reinterpret the cosilting mutation introduced in arXiv:2201.02147.

</details>


### [103] [Double Transposed Poisson Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01066)
*Maxime Fairon,Nikita Safonkin*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文介绍了双转置泊松代数（double transposed Poisson algebras），这是Bai等人提出的转置泊松代数的非交换类比，与Kontsevich-Rosenberg原理兼容。作者首先考虑简化版本（id-adapted双转置泊松代数），然后探讨一般定义。证明了在单位结合代数上的每个此类结构由单个导子控制，该导子映射到张量积空间，并进一步在每个表示代数上诱导GL_N-等变转置泊松结构。还定义了H_0-转置泊松结构，并通过迹映射在GL_N-不变环上获得转置泊松结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将转置泊松代数推广到非交换设置，使其与Kontsevich-Rosenberg原理兼容，从而建立非交换几何与泊松结构之间的桥梁。

Method: 首先定义id-adapted双转置泊松代数作为简化版本，再推广到一般定义。利用导子映射到张量积空间的结构刻画，通过表示代数和迹映射构造等变和不变环上的转置泊松结构。

Result: 证明了双转置泊松结构由单个导子完全控制；在表示代数上诱导出GL_N-等变转置泊松结构；通过迹映射在GL_N-不变环上得到转置泊松结构。

Conclusion: 双转置泊松代数为非交换泊松几何提供了新的代数框架，与表示理论和不变理论自然联系。

Abstract: We introduce double transposed Poisson algebras, a noncommutative analogue of the transposed Poisson algebras of Bai, Bai, Guo and Wu that is compatible with the Kontsevich--Rosenberg principle. We first consider a simplified version which we call id-adapted double transposed Poisson algebras and then explore the general definition. We prove that every such structure on a unital associative algebra $\mathbb{A}$ is governed by a single derivation $\mathbb{A}\to\mathbb{A}\otimes\operatorname{S}(\mathbb{A}/[\mathbb{A},\mathbb{A}])$. Furthermore, this induces a $\operatorname{GL}_N$-equivariant transposed Poisson structure on each representation algebra $\mathbb{A}_N=\Bbbk[\operatorname{Rep}_N(\mathbb{A})]$. We also define $H_0$-transposed Poisson structures, the transposed counterpart of Crawley-Boevey's $H_0$-Poisson structures, and use the trace map to obtain a transposed Poisson structure on the ring of $\operatorname{GL}_N$-invariants $\mathbb{A}_N^{\operatorname{GL}_N}$.

</details>


### [104] [Semisimple types for quaternionic forms of p-adic classical groups and compatible beta-extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01074)
*Daniel Skodlerack,Shuyang Ye*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文针对p-adic古典群的四元数形式G（p为奇数），为G的平滑复表示范畴的每个Bernstein块构造了Bushnell-Kutzko-Stevens类型，并构建了一个由中心化子G_β的Bruhat-Tits建筑室点参数化且通过转移相关联的兼容β-扩张系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将Bushnell-Kutzko-Stevens类型理论推广到p-adic古典群的四元数形式，并建立兼容的β-扩张系统，以更深入地理解这类群的表示理论结构。

Method: 采用Bushnell-Kutzko-Stevens类型理论的框架，针对四元数形式的p-adic古典群，构造每个Bernstein块的类型；并通过Bruhat-Tits建筑理论，构建由中心化子G_β的室点参数化的兼容β-扩张族，建立转移关系。

Result: 成功为每个Bernstein块构造了Bushnell-Kutzko-Stevens类型，并构建了由G_β的Bruhat-Tits建筑室点参数化的兼容β-扩张系统，这些扩张通过转移相互关联。

Conclusion: 将Bushnell-Kutzko-Stevens类型理论推广到四元数形式的p-adic古典群，并建立了系统化的β-扩张框架，为后续研究这类群的表示提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a quaternionic form of a $p$-adic classical group ($p$ odd). We construct a Bushnell-Kutzko-Stevens type for every Bernstein block of the category of smooth complex representations of $G$. Further we construct a system of compatible $β$-extensions, i.e. a family of $β$-extensions parametrised by the points of a chamber of the Bruhat-Tits building of the centralizer $G_β$ which are related via transfer.

</details>

### [105] [Dissipation splits the Mott transition in one dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00086)
*Oscar Bouverot-Dupuis,Alberto Rosso,Laura Foini*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究耗散如何改变一维Mott相变。通过玻色化和精确积分浴自由度，发现当浴指数s<3/2时，系统不会发生从Luttinger液体到Mott绝缘体的直接转变，而是出现一个中间耗散相——可压缩、无能隙但超流刚度为0。原本的Mott相变分裂为两个不同的临界现象：LL到耗散相的BKT转变，以及耗散相到MI的新的公度-非公度转变。对于1<s<3/2，临界指数随浴指数连续变化；对于s<1，掺杂呈超幂律衰减。蒙特卡洛模拟定量支持这些预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解耗散如何修改量子相变是多体物理学的核心挑战。一维Mott转变是典型范例，在孤立系统中它将导电的Luttinger液体与Mott绝缘体分开，但耗散的影响尚不清楚。

Method: 使用玻色化方法和对浴自由度的精确积分，结合蒙特卡洛模拟。

Result: 发现浴指数s<3/2时出现中间耗散相，Mott转变分裂为两个临界现象；1<s<3/2时临界指数持续变化，s<1时掺杂呈超幂律衰减。

Conclusion: 耗散能够定性改变Mott相变的性质，并在强关联一维系统中产生新的临界行为。

Abstract: Understanding how dissipation modifies quantum phase transitions is a central challenge in many-body physics. A paradigmatic example is the one-dimensional Mott transition, which in isolated systems separates a conducting Luttinger liquid (LL) from a Mott insulator (MI). Here, we study the fate of this transition in the presence of dissipative baths locally coupled to the density. Using bosonisation and an exact integration of the bath degrees of freedom, we show that dissipation fundamentally reshapes the phase diagram for bath exponents $s<3/2$, where $s$ characterises the low-energy bath spectrum. Rather than undergoing a direct LL-MI transition, the system develops an intermediate dissipative phase (DP) that is compressible and gapless, yet has zero superfluid stiffness. As a result, the conventional Mott transition splits into two distinct critical phenomena: a Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless transition from the LL to the DP, followed by a new commensurate-incommensurate transition from the DP to the MI. We derive an effective field theory for the latter transition and characterize its universality. For $1<s<3/2$, the critical exponents vary continuously with the bath exponent as $β=ν=1/z=s-1$, while for $s<1$ the transition is governed by $β=ν=1/z=0$ and the doping vanishes sharper than any power law. State-of-the-art Monte Carlo simulations quantitatively support our predictions. These results demonstrate that dissipation can qualitatively alter the nature of the Mott transition and generate novel critical behaviour in strongly correlated one-dimensional systems.

</details>


### [106] [Phase distinction of Gibbs states without symmetry breaking: topological invariants of the 3D toric code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00134)
*Haruki Watanabe*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究三维Z₂环面码在有限温度和对称性破缺磁场下的拓扑序，发现拓扑纠缠熵在拓扑相中保持量子化γ=ln2，但在热转变时坍缩为0。更重要的是，作者引入了解码威尔逊环关联f_W作为混合态中准局部信道不变的鲁棒拓扑不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究三维Z₂环面码在有限温度下以及存在对称性破缺磁场时，拓扑序是否还能保持，并寻找混合态中真正的鲁棒拓扑不变量。

Method: 微扰分析和大规模量子蒙特卡洛模拟，计算拓扑纠缠熵和解码威尔逊环关联，并检验其对准局部信道的响应。

Result: 拓扑纠缠熵在拓扑相中保持γ=ln2的量子化值（由Bianchi恒等式几何保护），在热转变时坍缩为0；但该值可通过常深度信道从平凡态生成。解码威尔逊环关联f_W在拓扑相中量子化为1，在平凡相中为0，且是准局部信道不变量。

Conclusion: 混合态拓扑序需要比拓扑纠缠熵更鲁棒的诊断工具，解码威尔逊环关联f_W可作为混合态中拓扑序的可靠不变量。

Abstract: We study the finite-temperature topological order of the three-dimensional $\mathbb{Z}_2$ toric code in a generic magnetic field, where every higher-form symmetry is explicitly broken and can at most be emergent. We show perturbatively, and confirm by large-scale quantum Monte Carlo, that the topological entanglement entropy stays quantized at $γ= \ln 2$ throughout the topological phase -- at finite temperature and under the symmetry-breaking field alike -- and collapses to $0$ across the thermal transition, a quantization protected geometrically by the Bianchi identity rather than by any exact symmetry of the system. The plateau $γ= \ln 2$ is, however, not invariant under quasi-local channels: a constant-depth channel can generate this identical quantized value from a trivial product state. We therefore introduce the decoded Wilson-loop correlation $f_W$, which quantizes to $1$ in the topological phase and $0$ in the trivial phase as $L\to\infty$ and, unlike $γ$, is a quasi-local-channel invariant -- a robust topological invariant of the mixed state.

</details>


### [107] [Holographic Quantum Transformer: A Generalist Neuro-Symbolic Architecture for Solving Frustrated Systems via Generative Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00398)
*Xingran Guo,Tiaojie Xiao,Jie Liu,Keqin Li*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出全息量子变换器（HQT），一种基于生成式自注意力的架构，用于模拟二维阻挫量子物质。在$J_1-J_2$海森堡模型上，HQT在$8\times8$阻挫点达到$E/N=-0.5001(1)$的精度，并提出了“全息迁移”零样本尺寸外推协议，将$8\times8$训练模型直接迁移至$10\times10$晶格，无需重新训练即可达到$E/N=-0.49782(3)$的变分最优能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维阻挫量子物质模拟面临符号问题和指数级希尔伯特空间复杂度的巨大挑战，现有方法难以高效处理非局域纠缠模式和大尺寸系统的可迁移模拟。

Method: 提出全息量子变换器（HQT）架构，利用全局自注意力机制解析非局域纠缠模式；开发“全息迁移”零样本尺寸外推协议，通过连续位置嵌入插值和头部重初始化，将小晶格训练模型直接投影到大晶格。

Result: 在$8\times8$阻挫量子临界点$J_2=0.5$处达到$E/N=-0.5001(1)$；零样本迁移至$10\times10$晶格得到$E/N=-0.49782(3)$，与变分最先进方法统计一致；注意力图谱可解释地恢复了$J_2$相互作用几何。

Conclusion: 生成式自注意力作为可扩展的范式可用于可迁移量子模拟，全息迁移协议实现了跨晶格尺寸的零样本高效初始化与快速收敛。

Abstract: Simulating two-dimensional frustrated quantum matter is a grand challenge due to the sign problem and exponential Hilbert space complexity. In this work, we introduce the Holographic Quantum Transformer (HQT), a physics-inspired generative architecture that leverages global self-attention to resolve non-local entanglement patterns. We validate HQT on the square lattice $J_1-J_2$ Heisenberg model. On the heavily frustrated $8 \times 8$ lattice at the quantum critical point ($J_2=0.5$), HQT reaches a ground-state energy per site ($E/N$) of $\mathbf{-0.5001(1)}$, consistent with the expected finite-size scaling trend. Beyond numerical accuracy, HQT exhibits intrinsic physical awareness, autonomously recovering the underlying $J_2$ interaction geometry through interpretable attention maps. Our central contribution is ``Holographic Transfer", a zero-shot size-extrapolation protocol with rapid alignment: a model trained on $8 \times 8$ systems is directly projected onto larger $10 \times 10$ lattices via continuous positional-embedding interpolation and head re-initialization, achieving high-fidelity initialization and rapid convergence. This zero-shot protocol yields an energy of $E/N = \mathbf{-0.49782(3)}$, statistically consistent with the variational state of the art while requiring no from-scratch training on the target lattice. Our results establish generative attention as a scalable paradigm for transferable quantum simulation.

</details>


### [108] [Weak Ferromagnetism in NiS$_2$ under Nanocrystallization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00474)
*Hayato Miyazaki,Tomohiko Yoshinaga,Ojiro Miyazaki,Akira Matsuo,Koichi Kindo,Hirofumi Ishii,Tatsuya Kawae,Tetsuya Kida,Masashi Nantoh,Yoichi Ishiwata*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: NiS₂纳米晶体（~27 nm）保持体材料的反铁磁转变，但在低温下表现出铁磁滞后、大矫顽力、交换偏压和垂直回线偏移，归因于表面未补偿磁矩，这排斥了均匀倾斜模型而支持畴壁机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究NiS₂纳米晶体的磁性质，特别是表面效应对反铁磁有序的影响，以及利用表面未补偿磁矩来区分体材料弱铁磁性的不同理论模型。

Method: 通过磁化强度和比热测量，表征直径约27 nm的结构有序NiS₂纳米晶体的磁性质，分析其在不同温度下的磁滞回线和场冷却效应。

Result: 纳米晶体保留了体材料的两步反铁磁转变；低温下表现出铁磁滞后、大矫顽力、交换偏压和垂直偏移；高温区接近线性响应；未观察到额外的体材料弱铁磁成分。

Conclusion: NiS₂纳米晶体的磁行为由表面未补偿磁矩主导，该结果排除了均匀倾斜模型，间接支持体材料弱铁磁性的畴壁机制。

Abstract: Structurally well-ordered NiS$_2$ nanocrystals with an average diameter of $27.0 \pm 6.5$ nm retain the bulk-like two-step antiferromagnetic transitions, as shown by magnetization and heat-capacity measurements. Below the lower transition, the nanocrystals exhibit a hysteretic ferromagnetic response with large coercivity, exchange bias, and a vertical loop shift after field cooling, whereas the $M$-$H$ response just above the transition is nearly linear. These features are best explained by uncompensated surface moments generated where the low-temperature antiferromagnetic order terminates at the nanocrystal surface. The absence of a clear additional bulk-like weak-ferromagnetic component constrains homogeneous-canting models and indirectly favors a domain-wall scenario for the weak ferromagnetism of bulk NiS$_2$.

</details>


### [109] [Strongly frustrated 2D magnetism in a 3D hexagonal perovskite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00532)
*Bocheng Yu,Otkur Omar,Songtai Lv,Long Ma,Zhengcai Xia,Jing Meng,Yanran Yang,Jie Ma,Yang Xu,Qingfeng Zhan,Vladimir Yu. Pomjakushin,Haiyuan Zou,Shang Gao,Toni Shiroka,Tian Shang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究通过核磁共振、中子散射和μ子自旋光谱等多种技术，发现Ba₂La₂MnTe₂O₁₂（一种三角晶格磁体）在约4.4 K发生磁相变，形成面内120°反铁磁序而c轴无序的准二维磁性基态，表现出强阻挫特性，挑战了对其他三维六方钙钛矿磁有序的解释，并揭示了维度对奇异磁态的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索低维磁性系统中可能存在的奇异量子现象，寻找强阻挫二维磁性的真实实例，并理解维度如何影响奇异磁态的形成。

Method: 结合核磁共振（NMR）、中子散射（Neutron Scattering）和μ子自旋光谱（μSR）三种实验技术，对Ba₂La₂MnTe₂O₁₂六方钙钛矿进行系统的磁性表征。

Result: Ba₂La₂MnTe₂O₁₂在TN≈4.4 K发生磁转变，形成面内120°反铁磁序，而c轴方向保持无序；零场μSR显示持续强自旋涨落和大内场分布；该体系的阻挫程度远高于多数已知磁有序阻挫系统。

Conclusion: 该工作挑战了对其他三维六方钙钛矿磁有序的传统解释，揭示了维度对奇异磁态的调控作用，为理解二维磁性及强阻挫体系提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Exotic quantum phenomena are often found to occur in spin systems that exhibit low-dimensional magnetism. By combining nuclear magnetic resonance, neutron scattering, and muon-spin spectroscopy ($μ$SR) techniques, we report a rare instance of strongly frustrated two-dimensional (2D) magnetism in a three-dimensional (3D) hexagonal perovskite. Here, Ba$_2$La$_2$MnTe$_2$O$_{12}$, a triangular-lattice magnet, is shown to undergo a magnetic transition at $T_\mathrm{N} \approx$ 4.4 K, below which the manganese moments form a 120$^{\circ}$ AFM order within the $ab$-plane, while staying disordered along the $c$-axis. This exotic ground state, which exhibits ideal 2D magnetism, is highly consistent with the persistently strong spin fluctuations and the large internal field distributions revealed by zero-field $μ$SR. Further, the 2D magnetism also leads to a significant frustration, much larger than that of most known magnetically-ordered frustrated systems. Our work on Ba$_2$La$_2$MnTe$_2$O$_{12}$ not only challenges the interpretations of magnetic order in other 3D hexagonal perovskites, but it also provides insight into how the dimensionality affects the exotic magnetic states.

</details>


### [110] [Quantum Oscillations of $\mathrm{Sr}_2\mathrm{RuO}_4$ under c-Axis Uniaxial Stress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00679)
*Fabian Jerzembeck,Maximilian T. Pelly,Helge Rosner,Edgar Abarca Morales,Naoki Kikugawa,Dmitry A. Sokolov,Andrew P. Mackenzie,Andreas W. Rost,Elena Hassinger,Javier F. Landaeta*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文首次在非常规超导体Sr₂RuO₄中实现了c轴单轴应力下的de Haas-van Alphen (dHvA)测量，直接研究了电子结构随应力的演化，揭示了空穴型α和电子型β、γ费米面面积的变化，发现了电荷转移是接近Lifshitz转变和范霍夫奇点的机制，并观察到有效质量轻微增强，实验结果与磁致伸缩量子振荡及能带计算吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单轴应力已被广泛用于关联电子材料研究，但缺乏费米面分辨的电子结构演化实验数据，特别是尚无单轴应力下dHvA效应的报道。本文旨在通过dHvA测量直接研究c轴单轴应力下电子结构的演化，并解释电子态密度增强与Tc抑制之间的矛盾行为。

Method: 在Sr₂RuO₄中施加c轴单轴应力（最高-1.8 GPa），进行dHvA测量以追踪所有费米面的演化，结合磁致伸缩量子振荡和能带结构计算进行对比分析。

Result: 发现空穴型α片面积增大，电子型β片面积减小，而电子型γ片面积增大；所有三个费米面上的有效质量在接近Lifshitz转变时轻微增强；dHvA结果与磁致伸缩量子振荡及能带计算吻合良好。

Conclusion: 与面内单轴应力不同，c轴单轴应力下电荷转移是接近电子-空穴Lifshitz转变及相关范霍夫奇点的机制。量子振荡测量结合单轴应力与能带计算是研究量子材料的一种有前景的新方法。

Abstract: Uniaxial stress has now been widely used to study correlated electron materials. However, Fermi surface-resolved experimental data on the evolution of the electronic structure under piezoelectrically applied stress are sparse, with no reports of de Haas-van Alphen (dHvA) effects under uniaxial stress. Here we present dHvA measurements under $c$-axis uniaxial stress on the unconventional superconductor $\mathrm{Sr}_2\mathrm{RuO}_4$. This allows us to study the evolution of the electronic structure directly and to gain insight into the contradicting behavior of the predicted enhancement of the electronic density of states and the observed suppression of $T_\text{c}$. We are able to follow all Fermi surfaces for stress up to $-1.8$~GPa and find that the cross-sectional areas of the hole-like $α$ sheet increase and electron-like $β$ sheet decrease. At the same time, the area of the electron-like $γ$ sheet increases. Therefore, in contrast to in-plane uniaxial stress, charge transfer is the mechanism for approaching the electron-to-hole Lifshitz transition and the associated Van Hove singularity. Additionally, we find that the effective masses on all three Fermi sheets are slightly enhanced as the Lifshitz transition is approached. We compare the dHvA results with quantum oscillations in the magnetostriction and band structure calculations, and find good agreement. At a more general level, our findings show that quantum oscillation measurements under uniaxial stress, combined with band-structure calculations, offer a promising new route for studying quantum materials.

</details>


### [111] [Weak-coupling tensor cross interpolation impurity solver for nonequilibrium dynamical mean-field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00702)
*Shuta Matsuura,Hiroshi Shinaoka,Philipp Werner,Naoto Tsuji*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于弱耦合展开和张量交叉插值(TCI)的非平衡杂质求解器，将其应用于非平衡动力学平均场理论(DMFT)。该方法以张量列形式近似弱耦合展开中的高维积分被积函数，无需随机采样即可高效计算，缓解了连续时间量子蒙特卡洛(CT-QMC)方法中的符号问题。在精确可解模型和Hubbard模型上的基准测试表明，该方法在半满时与CT-QMC精度相当，在半满以外符号问题严重时仍保持良好控制，并能应用于稳态DMFT问题获得准确的谱函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟非平衡量子多体系统由于计算复杂度随实时指数增长而极具挑战，现有的CT-QMC方法受符号问题严重限制，亟需开发能有效缓解符号问题的高精度非平衡杂质求解器。

Method: 基于弱耦合展开与张量交叉插值(TCI)，将高维积分被积函数近似为张量列形式，从而无需随机抽样即可高效求积，规避了CT-QMC的符号问题。

Result: 在精确可解非平衡杂质模型上结果与精确解吻合，揭示了被积函数的低秩结构；在半满Hubbard模型的相互作用淬火问题中，该方法以与CT-QMC相当的精度重现了临界相互作用强度下的快速热化；在远离半满（如3/4填充）时该方法仍保持良好控制，揭示了平滑交叉行为而非尖锐的快速热化点；在稳态DMFT中无需解析延拓即可获得金属态下的准确谱函数。

Conclusion: 该TCI-based非平衡杂质求解器有效克服了CT-QMC的符号问题，在半满及远离半满情形下均能稳定工作，为强关联非平衡量子多体系统的模拟提供了一种高效、可控的新方法。

Abstract: Simulating nonequilibrium quantum many-body systems remains a major challenge due to the exponential growth of the computational complexity with real time. Here we implement a nonequilibrium impurity solver based on the weak-coupling expansion and the tensor cross interpolation (TCI), and apply it to nonequilibrium dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT). The method approximates the integrands of the high-dimensional integrals arising in the weak-coupling expansion in a tensor-train form, enabling efficient evaluations without stochastic sampling and thereby mitigating the sign problem affecting continuous-time quantum Monte Carlo (CT-QMC) methods. Benchmark calculations for an exactly solvable nonequilibrium impurity model agree well with the exact results and reveal a low-rank structure of the integrands. When applied to interaction-quench problems in the half-filled Hubbard model, the method reproduces fast thermalization at a critical interaction strength with accuracy comparable to CT-QMC. Away from half filling, where the sign problem becomes even more severe, the present approach remains well controlled, revealing a crossover instead of a sharply defined fast thermalization point in the 3/4-filled case. The solver can also be applied to steady-state DMFT problems, yielding accurate spectral functions in the metallic regime without analytic continuation.

</details>


### [112] [Deconfined criticality between an antiferromagnetic insulator and a nodal d-wave superconductor: a quantum Monte Carlo study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00762)
*Chuang Chen,Subir Sachdev,Zi Yang Meng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过量子蒙特卡洛研究，在半填充正方格子上发现了从绝缘尼尔态到节线d波超导体的二级去禁闭量子相变，两种有序相均连续消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强关联电子体系中莫特绝缘体与d波超导体之间的量子相变性质，特别是相变级次及序参量演化行为，解决此前直接研究电子自由度时存在的争议。

Method: 采用部分子表示（费米子自旋子和玻色子荷子），两者在π通量背景中运动并耦合到量子涨落SU(2)晶格规范场，通过量子蒙特卡洛模拟研究无符号问题的阻挫磁有序区域。

Result: 发现二级去禁闭量子相变，尼尔序和d波超导序均连续消失；自旋子-荷子复合算符（电子量子数）在d波超导相中呈现无质量狄拉克色散，在反铁磁相中变为有质量能隙色散。

Conclusion: 部分子-规范场方法揭示了费米子自旋子和玻色子荷子之间存在一种超越朗道-金兹堡范式的去禁闭量子临界性，为理解铜氧化物超导体的相图提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We present a quantum Monte Carlo study of the transition between the insulating Néel state and the nodal $d$-wave superconductor on the square lattice at half-filling. We access a regime of frustrated magnetic order without a sign problem using a parton representation of the electron in terms of fermionic spinons and bosonic chargons. Both partons move in a background $π$-flux (so the electron experiences no net flux) and are coupled to a quantum fluctuating SU(2) lattice gauge field. In contrast to earlier studies directly on the electronic degrees of freedom, we find evidence for a second-order deconfined quantum phase transition at which both the Néel and $d$-wave superconductivity orders vanish continuously. We compute correlators of the spinon-chargon composite with the same quantum numbers as the electron: we find a gapless Dirac dispersion inside the $d$-wave superconductor, turning into a gapped dispersion in the antiferromagnet.

</details>


### [113] [Tensor Network Solvers for Ultra-large Tight-binding Hamiltonians: Algorithms and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00991)
*Tiago V. C. Antão,Anouar Moustaj,Yitao Sun,Jose L. Lado*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一的张量网络方法，通过将N=2^L个格点系统映射为L个赝自旋的多体问题，并用量子张量交叉插值构建任意跃迁函数，无需显式矩阵存储即可求解超大尺寸（数十亿格点）的紧束缚问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在介观乃至宏观尺度下理解量子材料需要紧束缚计算，但系统尺寸增大时显式矩阵表示的计算成本过高，成为研究莫尔、超莫尔异质结和准晶等现象的主要瓶颈。

Method: 将N=2^L个格点系统映射为L个赝自旋的多体问题，用张量网络算法求解；利用量子张量交叉插值构建任意跃迁函数（包括长程、空间调制、扭曲层耦合）；所有物理量通过张量网络代数计算，无需显式矩阵存储或对角化。

Result: 张量网络键维数仅需几十阶，与N无关；可常规求解数十亿格点系统；实现了谱函数、动量空间谱、实空间拓扑不变量、实时动力学、自洽平均场对称性破缺、非厄米现象和激子多体物理等应用。

Conclusion: 该框架利用实空间结构的张量网络可压缩性，建立了一种灵活的超大尺度量子物质研究方法，并已在开源Julia包TensorBinding.jl中实现。

Abstract: Understanding quantum materials at meso and even macroscopic scales requires tight-binding calculations on system sizes where explicit matrix representations become prohibitively costly. This represents a major bottleneck to rationalize phenomena in moiré and super-moiré heterostructures and quasicrystals. Here, we present a unified tensor-network methodology to solve tight-binding problems at exceptionally large scales, by mapping a system of $N = 2^L$ sites onto a many-body problem of $L$ pseudospin sites, which is subsequently solved with tensor network algorithms. For Hamiltonians with compressible real-space structure, the tensor network bond dimension remains modest, typically of order a few tens, independent of $N$.Tensor network representations of arbitrary hopping functions including long-range, spatially modulated, and twisted-layer couplings are built with quantics tensor cross interpolation, and all physical observables are evaluated entirely with tensor network algebra without explicit matrix storage or diagonalization. We demonstrate applications to spectral functions, momentum-space spectra via the tensor-network quantum Fourier transform, real-space topological invariants, real-time dynamics, correlation induced symmetry breaking with self-consistent mean-field calculations, non-Hermitian phenomena, and excitonic many-body physics. Our methodology enables routinely solving systems with billions of sites, by leveraging the tensor network compressibility of real-space structures, and establishing a flexible framework to study quantum matter at ultra-large length scales. The methodology is implemented in the open-source Julia package TensorBinding.jl.

</details>


### [114] [Exact interlayer triplet-pairing eigenstates in the extended Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01038)
*F. X. Liu,Z. Song*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文将$η$-配对对称性推广至三重态配对，提出多层扩展Hubbard模型中的层间三重态配对算符，发现了一组具有非对角长程序的确切凝聚对本征态，该机制源于受限谱生成代数，仅适用于存在层间Hubbard相互作用的双层和三层系统，且系统中单重态和三重态配对共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $η$-配对对称性在存在层间相互作用时被破坏，本文旨在将该方法推广至三重态配对，探索多层Hubbard模型中是否存在类似的确切凝聚对态。

Method: 提出了多层扩展Hubbard模型中的层间三重态配对算符，通过受限谱生成代数构造确切凝聚对本征态，并采用淬火动力学进行数值模拟验证。

Result: 发现了确切的凝聚对态具有非对角长程序；该机制仅适用于存在层间Hubbard相互作用的双层和三层系统；系统中单重态和三重态配对共存于本征态中。

Conclusion: 该工作为强关联系统中确切凝聚对态的研究开辟了新途径。

Abstract: $η$-pairing symmetry generalizes the pairing mechanisms in superconductivity but is broken in the presence of interlayer interactions. In this work, we extend this approach to triplet pairs. We propose interlayer triplet-pairing operators for the multi-layer extended Hubbard model. We find that a set of exact condensate-pair eigenstates can be constructed, which exhibit off-diagonal long-range order. In contrast to the $η$-pairing mechanism, this originates from restricted spectrum generating algebra and is only available for bilayer and trilayer systems in the presence of interlayer Hubbard interactions. Nevertheless, the system also retains the original on-site $η$-pairing symmetry in the absence of interlayer interactions. Consequently, both singlet and triplet pairs coexist in the eigenstates of the multi-layer Hubbard model. We employ quench dynamics to demonstrate the results through numerical simulations. Our findings open avenues for the study of exact condensate-pair states in strongly correlated systems.

</details>


### [115] [Chiral enhancement of two-magnon bound states in an $S=1/2$ triangular-lattice magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01062)
*László Rudner,Karlo Penc*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究三角晶格J1-J2-J3海森堡模型在标量手性相互作用下的一磁子和双磁子激发，发现标量手性在单磁子扇区抵消，但在双磁子扇区作为定向相互作用增强双磁子束缚态，提供了一种不改变单磁子谱而增强双磁子束缚的微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索标量手性相互作用对自旋-1/2三角晶格海森堡模型中磁子激发的影响，特别是验证标量手性是否能作为增强双磁子束缚而不影响单磁子谱的微观机制。

Method: 通过将单磁子色散改写为完全平方形式识别最小流形；利用对称性适配的三角晶格谐函数，在Γ点推导对称分辨的A1和E2类分波通道的有限维隙方程；结合精确对角化验证。

Result: 标量手性在单磁子扇区完全抵消，不影响单磁子色散与单磁子不稳定性；在双磁子扇区作为相邻翻转自旋间的定向相互作用，在E2型通道中分裂两种相对运动手性，选择性增强一种双磁子束缚态通道；在M点和不可公度总动量处也发现额外束缚态。

Conclusion: 标量手性是增强双磁子束缚的高效微观机制，在不移动单磁子谱的前提下增强束缚，为高场自旋向列相和多极不稳定性提供路径。

Abstract: We study one- and two-magnon excitations above the fully polarized state of the spin-$1/2$ triangular-lattice $J_1$-$J_2$-$J_3$ Heisenberg model with an additional uniform scalar-chirality interaction. In the one-magnon sector of the Heisenberg model, we identify two special minimum manifolds by rewriting the dispersion in complete-square form. The scalar-chirality term cancels exactly in this sector, leaving the one-magnon dispersion and the single-magnon instability unchanged. In contrast, it survives in the two-magnon sector as an oriented interaction between neighboring flipped spins. Using symmetry-adapted triangular-lattice harmonics, we derive finite-dimensional gap equations at the $Γ$ point in the symmetry-resolved $\mathsf{A_1}$ and $\mathsf{E_2}$-type partial-wave channels. The chirality coupling splits the two opposite relative-motion chiralities in the $\mathsf{E_2}$-type sector, thereby selectively enhancing one two-magnon bound-state channel. Exact diagonalization confirms this mechanism and reveals enhanced binding, as well as additional bound states at $M$ and at incommensurate total momenta. Our results identify scalar chirality as an efficient microscopic mechanism for strengthening two-magnon binding without shifting the one-magnon spectrum, and provide a route toward high-field spin-nematic and multipolar instabilities.

</details>


### [116] [Exact dimer ground states of long-range spin chains and ladders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01081)
*Jędrzej Wardyn,Miłosz Panfil*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了一大类相互作用的自旋链和梯子模型，确定了精确二聚体态成为基态的明确条件，并通过精确对角化在Majumdar-Ghosh模型的多种推广中验证了这些条件，为包含各向异性和任意范围相互作用的宽泛自旋链和梯子模型提供了精确的相图参考点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相互作用的自旋链和梯子模型支持众多量子相，具有复杂的基态相图，需要精确的参考点来帮助理解这些系统的相图结构。

Method: 推导了保证精确二聚体态为基态的普适性条件，并利用精确对角化技术在Majumdar-Ghosh模型的各种推广形式中进行数值验证。

Result: 获得了精确二聚体态成为基态的明确、普适的条件，并在多种推广模型中验证了这些条件的有效性。

Conclusion: 这些结果为包含各向异性和任意范围相互作用的宽泛自旋链和梯子模型提供了精确的相图参考点。

Abstract: Interacting spin chains and ladders are known to support a plethora of quantum phases with complex ground-state phase diagrams. In this work, we study a large family of such models and determine precise, explicit conditions under which an exact dimer state is guaranteed to be the ground state. These general conditions are validated for various generalizations of the Majumdar-Ghosh model using exact diagonalization. Our results provide exact reference points in the phase diagrams of a wide class of spin chains and ladders, including those with anisotropic and arbitrary-range interactions.

</details>


### [117] [Strange Luttinger liquids in a cavity-embedded one-dimensional electronic chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01146)
*Danh-Phuong Nguyen,Christophe Mora,Cristiano Ciuti*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维电子链与均匀量子化真空场及电子-电子相互作用的耦合。在没有电子相互作用时，推导出低能有效描述为奇异Luttinger液体，其光-物质耦合改变了标准Luttinger液体的速度关系。考虑电子相互作用后，得到了包含马约拉纳零模在内的多相图，并通过精确对角化计算了腔场对相边界的移动效应及马约拉纳态的改变。最后探讨了奇异Luttinger液体描述在引入电子相互作用后是否仍然成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究一维电子系统在耦合量子化真空场（腔光子系统）下的新奇量子相行为，特别是光-物质耦合如何改变标准Luttinger液体理论，以及能否诱导出马约拉纳零模等拓扑物态。

Method: 低能有效场论推导（博戈柳博夫变换等方法）结合精确对角化数值计算。

Result: 无电子相互作用时得到奇异Luttinger液体描述，打破了标准速度关系；有相互作用时得到包含马约拉纳零模的相图；腔场显著移动相边界并修改马约拉纳态性质。

Conclusion: 光-物质耦合从根本上改变了Luttinger液体的低能行为，并在相互作用存在时产生新的量子相；奇异Luttinger液体描述在引入电子相互作用后是否成立仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: We study a one-dimensional electronic chain coupled to a homogeneous quantized vacuum field and electron-electron interactions. In the absence of the latter, we derive a low-energy effective description in the presence of light-matter coupling, which we identify as a strange Luttinger liquid. Although it retains a formal resemblance to conventional Luttinger liquid theory, the coupling to the quantum field qualitatively modifies the low-energy sector and breaks the standard velocity relation underlying Luttinger universality. For finite electron-electron interactions, we recover a phase diagram featuring several phases as a function of interaction strength and hopping amplitude, including a phase hosting Majorana-like zero modes. Using exact diagonalization, we compute observables that characterize the phase boundaries and show that the cavity field significantly shifts them. We also study the fate of Majorana-like states under the influence of the cavity field, highlighting their modification by light-matter coupling. Finally, we investigate whether the strange Luttinger liquid description identified in the noninteracting regime continues to hold when electron-electron interactions are introduced.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [EmbodimentSemantic: A Spatial Scene-Graph Dataset and Benchmark for Vision-Language Models on Embodied Manipulation Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00020)
*Hassan Jaber,Refinath S N,Luca Cagliero,Christopher E. Mower,Haitham Bou-Ammar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 EmbodimentSemantic，一个空间场景图数据集和基准，用于评估具身操作中的空间接地能力。数据包含真实机器人观测和仿真 LIBERO 基准（6万帧、12万+场景图），实验表明当前 VLM 在深度感知和视角依赖的空间结构上仍存在不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 系统能识别物体和遵循语言指令，但缺乏对物体空间排列（支撑、包含、顺序、遮挡、深度关系）的显式表示，空间接地是制约操作性能的关键瓶颈。

Method: 1) 构建 EmbodimentSemantic 场景图数据集，用有向物体-关系-物体三元组表示空间关系；2) 引入基于 MuJoCo 的 LIBERO 仿真基准，自动生成带真实标注的场景图；3) 将场景图注入 VLA 策略提示进行下游控制测试。

Result: 当前开源和商业 VLM 能预测合理的空间关系，但在精确的深度感知和视角相关的空间结构上表现不佳。

Conclusion: EmbodimentSemantic 为诊断 VLM 空间接地能力和测试 VLA 操作中的实用性提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Spatial grounding remains a key limitation of vision-language-action (VLA) systems for robotic manipulation. While current models can recognize objects and follow language instructions, they often lack an explicit representation of how objects are arranged in space, including support, containment, ordering, occlusion, and depth-sensitive relations. We introduce EmbodimentSemantic, a spatial scene-graph dataset and benchmark for evaluating relational grounding in embodied manipulation. EmbodimentSemantic represents scenes as directed object-relation-object triplets, where each triplet specifies a spatial relation between an ordered pair of objects using a fixed set of relations. This representation enables direct evaluation of object binding, relation prediction, and spatial consistency. The dataset includes real-world manipulation observations collected with the low-cost SO101 robot arm, together with generated scene graphs for studying spatial grounding in practical robotic settings. To provide controlled validation, we also introduce a simulator-grounded LIBERO benchmark with over 60K manipulation frames and more than 120K camera-specific scene graphs across paired third-person and wrist views, where ground-truth relations are derived automatically from MuJoCo geometry, world coordinates, camera projections, and visibility constraints. We further test whether scene graphs improve downstream control by injecting them into existing VLA policy prompts. Experiments across open-source and commercial VLMs show that current models often predict plausible relations but struggle with exact depth-aware and viewpoint-dependent spatial structure. EmbodimentSemantic provides a unified framework for diagnosing spatial grounding in VLM perception and testing its utility for VLA manipulation.

</details>


### [119] [When to Personalize Household Object Search: A Rigidity-Gated Hybrid Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00022)
*Xianyao Li,Yuhai Wang,Hu Xiao,Kaleb Smith,Gilbert Yang Ye,Eric Jing Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种刚性门控混合策略PerSim，仅在物体放置行为可变时进行个性化，通过合成数据模拟和Big Five人格特质预测室内先验，在减少服务机器人搜索成本方面取得小但显著的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人搜索家居物品依赖空间先验，但物体位置随居民特质变化；收集纵向、特质特定的居家轨迹具有侵入性且难以规模化。

Method: 提出PerSim：刚性门控混合策略（特质条件先验+人口频率基线），仅对低刚性物体个性化；构建人类校准的仿真流水线生成物体放置过渡数据；训练以连续Big Five五大人格向量为输入的预测器，输出房间级先验和房间内共现线索。

Result: 双层次人类验证(N=200)：合成过渡行为逼真度均值3.85/5(p<1e-6)；低刚性物体个性化更受青睐(p=0.005)；离线测试中，对未见连续特质向量有小但显著改进(p=0.035)；数字孪生中端到端搜索成本降低。

Conclusion: PerSim通过刚性门控决策规则决定何时个性化，结合合成数据与特质条件预测，在服务机器人家居物品搜索中实现端到端收益。

Abstract: Service robots searching for household objects rely on spatial priors to reduce search cost, yet object locations can vary with resident traits. Collecting longitudinal, trait-specific in-home trajectories is invasive and hard to scale. We study when personalization helps and propose PerSim, a rigidity-gated hybrid policy that combines a trait-conditioned prior with a population-frequency baseline, personalizing only when placement behavior is variable. To scale resident-conditioned dynamics, we employ a human-calibrated simulation pipeline to generate and validate object-placement transitions in diverse home layouts, and train a predictor that injects continuous Big Five vectors to output room-level priors and within-room co-occurrence cues. In a unified human study (N=200), dual-layer validation shows that (i) synthetic transitions are judged behaviorally plausible (mean 3.85/5, p < 1e-6), and (ii) in a blinded A/B comparison, personalization is favored primarily for low-rigidity objects (p=0.005), while the population-frequency baseline remains strong for universally placed items, yielding a decision rule for when to personalize. In an offline objective test, we observe a small but significant improvement on unseen continuous trait vectors over nearest discrete configuration matching (p=0.035), supporting interpolation in five-dimensional trait space. Finally, in a home digital twin we show that PerSim reduces expected search cost by combining room visitation effort with within-room cue checking, demonstrating end-to-end gains beyond isolated prediction metrics.

</details>


### [120] [Decentralized Geometric Control for Cable-Suspended Payload Transport with Adaptive Mass Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00024)
*Hadi Hajieghrary,Benedikt Walter,Paul Schmitt,Miguel Hurtado*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GPAC是一种四层分层架构，使多架四旋翼无人机无需中央协调器即可协同运输缆绳悬挂负载。通过隐式协调（每架无人机独立估计有效载荷分担）和局部测量实现协同，仅需低速率邻居位置广播用于碰撞避免。系统在完整非线性构型流形上运行，整合了几何控制、防摆调节、风扰观测器、并发学习质量估计和优先级排序的控制屏障函数安全滤波器。高保真仿真显示平均跟踪RMSE为33.8 cm（变异系数2.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同空中运输需要尊重非线性流形几何、无需集中协调并满足运行安全约束的控制器，现有方法在分布式几何控制、安全保证和计算效率方面存在不足。

Method: 提出GPAC四层分层架构：各无人机独立通过局部缆绳测量估计有效负载分担（隐式协调），局部重构负载位置；集成几何位置/姿态控制、防摆调节、扩展状态观测器（风扰抑制）、并发学习质量估计（无需持续激励）和优先级排序的CBF安全滤波器（具有输入到状态安全裕度）；证明滤波器力修改在SO(3)姿态控制器几乎全局稳定区域内。

Result: 高保真仿真（柔性缆绳、传感器融合、风湍流）在13个随机种子下平均负载跟踪RMSE为33.8 cm，变异系数2.8%，每代理计算成本低。

Conclusion: GPAC通过隐式协调和局部测量实现了分布式、安全、几何一致的协同空中运输，无需中央协调器、缆绳状态交换或自适应参数交换，且满足安全约束。

Abstract: Cooperative aerial transport requires controllers that respect nonlinear manifold geometry, operate without centralized coordination, and respect operational safety constraints. To address these demands, we present GPAC, a four-layer hierarchical architecture that enables $N$ quadrotors to transport a cable-suspended payload without a central coordinator or by exchanging cable states or adaptive parameters. The key insight is implicit coordination: each quadrotor independently estimates its effective load share from local cable measurements, so combined forces converge to the correct total, even without knowledge of $N$ or the payload mass; the payload position is reconstructed locally from each agent's own cable geometry, and the only inter-agent communication is a low-rate neighbor-position broadcast for collision avoidance. GPAC operates directly on the full nonlinear configuration manifold and integrates geometric position and attitude control, anti-swing regulation, an extended-state observer for wind rejection, concurrent learning-based mass estimation without persistent excitation, and a priority-ordered control barrier function (CBF)-inspired safety filter that reduces operational risk, with input-to-state safety (ISSf) margins that hold exactly under single-constraint activation. A compatibility result shows that the filter's force modifications keep the desired attitude within the almost-global stability region of the $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ attitude controller. Finally, high-fidelity simulation with flexible cables, onboard sensor fusion, and wind turbulence -- with all control and estimation loops closed through the estimator -- yields a mean payload-tracking RMSE of 33.8 cm (2.8\% coefficient of variation over 13 seeds) at a low per-agent computational cost.

</details>


### [121] [FLYNN: Robust Neural Network for Robot Navigation using Fly Brain Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00025)
*Benquan Wang,Jingdao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FLYNN——基于果蝇全脑连接组构建的循环神经网络，在视觉导航任务中性能媲美人工网络，且在分布外数据和感官缺失场景下表现出显著更强的鲁棒性，即使完全失去视觉输入仍能保持功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在新环境或感官剥夺条件下表现脆弱，而生物系统对此具有显著耐受性。受此启发，作者希望利用生物大脑的连接拓扑来构建更具鲁棒性的人工神经网络。

Method: 基于果蝇Drosophila melanogaster的突触分辨率脑连接组，直接推导出循环神经网络架构（FLYNN），并在MuJoCo环境中训练其执行基于视觉的导航任务。

Result: FLYNN在标准条件下性能与参数量相近的现代手工网络相当；在分布外数据和感官缺失场景下，FLYNN表现出显著更强的鲁棒性，即使完全失去视觉输入仍能维持功能；PCA分析表明FLYNN内部状态具有高度表征模块化，这可能与其鲁棒性相关。

Conclusion: 基于生物大脑连接拓扑设计的人工神经网络为构建更具韧性的智能体提供了新方向。

Abstract: While deep learning models achieve state-of-the-art performance in complex tasks, they remain brittle when faced with new environments or sensory deprivation. In contrast, biological systems exhibit remarkable tolerance to these challenges. We address this vulnerability by developing a recurrent neural network (RNN) whose architecture is directly derived from the synaptic-resolution brain connectome of the fruit fly Drosophila melanogaster. We demonstrate the feasibility of training the fly connectome neural network (FLYNN) to perform vision-based navigation in MuJoCo, achieving performance comparable to modern hand-crafted networks of similar parameter counts. Crucially, FLYNN exhibits superior resistance to out-of-distribution (OOD) data and tolerance to sensory loss without further training. It remained functional even under total vision loss while hand-crafted networks largely failed, even when specifically trained with camera dropout. Principal Component Analysis (PCA) of the internal state of FLYNN suggests that it exhibits a particularly high degree of representational modularity, which might be related to its robustness. Our work provides a new direction for designing resilient artificial agents following the topology of biological brains.

</details>


### [122] [Invariant Stochastic Filtering on SE(3) for Inertial-Encoder State Estimation of Serial Rigid Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00026)
*S. Yaqubi,J. Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对串行刚性机械臂状态估计问题，在SE(3)李群上开发了一种不变扩展卡尔曼滤波器(IEKF)，该滤波器利用运动学方程的群仿射性质使线性化误差动态自治，采用物理分离的噪声模型（陀螺仪和加速度计通道独立处理），并构建了模块化链式每链接IEKF结构，计算复杂度与链接数成线性关系，通过李代数Lyapunov函数证明了均方指数最终有界性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机械臂状态估计方法在处理非线性误差传播和计算复杂度方面存在局限性，需要一种能够精确描述误差协方差、物理上合理分离噪声源、且计算效率随机械臂自由度线性增长的滤波方法。

Method: 基于SE(3)李群的不变扩展卡尔曼滤波，利用群仿射性质使Riccati方程控制真实误差协方差；采用物理分离噪声模型，加速度计通过重力补偿积分提供平移扭转，其测量协方差与采样间隔成比例，陀螺仪噪声通过状态相关的科里奥利噪声项建模；构建模块化链式IEKF结构，每链接的预测协方差仅通过Adjoint变换的后验依赖于前驱链接。

Result: 通过李代数Lyapunov函数建立了均方指数最终有界性，每链接的界通过Adjoint算子范数链接，形成模块化且可扩展到任意链长的稳定性证书；数值结果验证了设计有效性。

Conclusion: 该IEKF设计在理论上保证了误差协方差的精确描述和全局稳定性，在计算上实现了线性复杂度，为多链接串行机械臂的状态估计提供了一种高效且理论完备的解决方案。

Abstract: An invariant extended Kalman filter (IEKF) is developed for state estimation of serial rigid manipulators with an arbitrary number of links, formulated entirely within the Lie group SE(3). The group-affine property of the kinematic equations makes the linearised error dynamics autonomous, so the Riccati equation governs the true error covariance rather than a local approximation. A physically separated noise model treats gyroscope and accelerometer channels independently: the accelerometer provides translational twist via gravity-compensated integration, yielding a measurement covariance that scales with the sample interval in exact analogy with process noise discretisation; a state-dependent Coriolis noise term captures gyroscope noise propagating through the nonlinear dynamics, vanishing at rest and growing with twist magnitude. The filter is structured as a modular chain of per-link IEKFs in which the predicted covariance of each link depends on its predecessor only through the Adjoint-transformed posterior, giving linear computational cost in link count. Exponential ultimate boundedness in mean square is established via a Lie algebra Lyapunov function, with per-link bounds chained through the Adjoint operator norm to yield a stability certificate that is modular and scalable to arbitrary chain length. Numerical results validate the design.

</details>


### [123] [Urban Deceleration Behavior Modes Under Scene Context: An Early-Kinematic Classifier from Argoverse 2 Multi-Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00027)
*Eni Solomon Laughter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文从真实城市驾驶数据中提取减速事件，通过聚类发现四种稳定的减速行为模式（预期柔和减速占62.8%、反应性接近减速占30.6%、刹车式急减速占4.8%及异常类），并分析了场景上下文对模式的影响，发现仅跟车对年龄具有中等效应，早期窗口的加加速度是主要预测信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市减速是跟车研究中实证广泛但缺乏系统分类的行为，现有研究未能从轨迹数据中有效发现减速模式。本文利用配备感知系统的自动驾驶数据集，以数据驱动的方式发现城市减速的行为模式，并量化场景上下文对模式的调节作用。

Method: 从Argoverse 2 Sensor数据集的234个城市驾驶日志中提取1,219个持续减速事件，将每个事件编码为19维运动学特征向量，采用K-means聚类结合bootstrap稳定性分析发现行为模式，并用11个场景上下文变量量化调节效应。使用HistGradientBoosting分类器从事件前1.0秒预测模式归属。

Result: 四种稳定模式（bootstrap调整兰德指数0.897）：预期柔和减速62.8%、反应性接近减速30.6%、刹车式急减速4.8%、异常类1.8%。仅跟车对年龄呈中等效应（epsilon²=0.085），场景几何和弱势道路使用者接近度效应可忽略。早期事件分类器在1.0秒时宏F1=0.758，场景上下文贡献+0.059 F1。模式在中速驾驶中具有不变性（ARI=0.817），在低速时依赖驾驶场景（ARI=0.166）。

Conclusion: 城市减速行为由少量稳定的运动学模式构成，早期窗口的加加速度是主要预测信号，跟车对年龄是首要的上下文调节因素。该发现对自动驾驶系统的行为预测和决策具有指导意义。

Abstract: Urban deceleration is one of the most empirically studied yet least taxonomically organized behaviors in car-following research. Recent perception-equipped autonomous-vehicle datasets enable trajectory-anchored mode discovery. We extract 1,219 sustained deceleration events from 234 urban driving logs of the Argoverse 2 Sensor dataset, encode each event in a 19-dimensional kinematic feature vector, discover behavioral modes via K-means clustering with bootstrap stability analysis, and quantify modulation by eleven scene-context variables. A HistGradientBoosting classifier predicts mode membership from the first 1.0 s of each event. Four stable modes emerge with a bootstrap Adjusted Rand Index of 0.897 across 50 resamples: anticipatory soft (62.8%), reactive closing (30.6%), brake-like jerk (4.8%), and an outlier category (1.8%). Only pair age shows a medium effect (epsilon^2 = 0.085); scene geometry and vulnerable-road-user proximity show negligible effects. The early-event classifier achieves macro-F1 = 0.758 at 1.0 s, with scene context contributing +0.059 F1 over kinematics alone. Modes are regime-invariant in medium-speed driving (ARI = 0.817) but regime-dependent at low speed (ARI = 0.166). A small set of stable kinematic modes structures urban deceleration; early-window jerk dominates predictive signal; and pair age is the primary contextual modulator.

</details>


### [124] [Trajectory Learning with Graph Representations for Social Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00028)
*Berke Kartal,Burcu Kilic,Yigit Yildirim,Emre Ugur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于模仿学习的自主导航框架，利用时空图注意力网络和轨迹级学习目标，实现社交合规的移动机器人导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法中，强化学习依赖手工设计的奖励函数，将社交行为简化为静态标准，无法复现真实行人行为模式；模仿学习缺乏社交交互建模且存在误差累积问题。因此需要一种能同时捕捉时空特征、建模社交交互并缓解误差累积的导航方法。

Method: 提出模仿学习框架，包含两个模块：(1) 基于图的辅助网络，通过注意力机制编码人群交互状态；(2) 导航模块，利用编码状态预测和轨迹级学习目标捕捉时间动态并缓解误差累积。

Result: 在仿真环境和真实数据集上，所提方法在多个社交指标上优于已有数据驱动基线方法。

Conclusion: 结合图注意力的时空建模与轨迹级模仿学习能有效提升移动机器人社交合规导航性能。

Abstract: Autonomous mobile robots are expected to exhibit socially compliant navigation for minimizing pedestrian disturbance. While capturing social interactions and incorporating pedestrian motion estimations into decision-making are beneficial for compliance, prior methods fail to address both spatial and temporal characteristics present in real-world data. Reinforcement Learning offers high capability, but it requires hand-crafted reward functions that reduce social behavior to static criteria, limiting its ability to reproduce patterns that exist in real pedestrian behavior. Imitation Learning offers direct training from real-world data but lacks modeling of social interactions and suffers from error accumulation. To this end, we propose an imitation learning framework that leverages spatiotemporal dynamics for socially compliant navigation. To represent social context based on interactions, we introduce a graph-based auxiliary network that encodes crowd states by attending to pedestrians. In addition, we present a navigation module that captures temporal dynamics and mitigates error accumulations by incorporating encoded state predictions and employing a trajectory-level learning objective. Our framework outperforms established data-driven baselines on simulation and a real-world dataset across diverse social metrics.

</details>


### [125] [Memory-Native Non-Terrestrial Networks for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00029)
*Chengyang Li,Yikun Wang,Jiahui He,Yujie Wan,Shuai Wang,Yuan Wu,Yik-Chung Wu,Chengzhong Xu,Huseyin Arslan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向具身智能的存算原生非地面网络（MemNTN）范式，通过双记忆架构（物理记忆+数字记忆）实现跨层长上下文决策优化，在卫星具身问答任务中显著超越传统无状态NTN方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NTN协议采用无状态设计，依赖局部信道条件和即时服务需求进行决策，无法适应具身智能场景下高度动态、资源受限、拓扑多变且面向任务的环境。

Method: 建立双记忆架构：物理记忆（表征世界状态）和数字记忆（编码历史网络经验）；设计记忆获取、压缩、估值、更新和利用机制，实现从物理/接入层到网络/应用层的跨层存算原生决策。

Result: 在卫星具身问答（SEQA）实验中，MemNTN显著优于传统无状态NTN方案和地面方法。

Conclusion: 引入长上下文记忆机制是提升NTN在具身智能场景下性能的关键范式转变，双记忆架构有效解决了无状态协议在动态环境中的局限性。

Abstract: Non-terrestrial networks (NTN) provide ubiquitous connectivity for embodied intelligence (EI), enabling robots in wilderness to leverage cloud resources or report critical information to remote centers. However, the synergy is nontrivial due to the highly-dynamic, resource-constrained, topology-varying, and task-oriented environment. Existing memoryless NTN protocols become inefficient, since the decisions are driven by local channel conditions and instantaneous service demands. To address these limitations, this paper proposes the memory-native NTN (MemNTN) paradigm that leverages long-horizon contexts for memory augmented system optimization. To realize this paradigm shift, we establish a dual-memory architecture that distinguishes between physical memory representing the state of the world and digital memory encoding historical network experience. We develop memory acquisition, compression, valuation, update, and utilization mechanisms that facilitate cross-layer, memory-native decision-making, spanning from the physical and access layers up to the network and application layers. Experiments in satellite embodied question answering (SEQA) demonstrate that the proposed MemNTN significantly outperforms conventional stateless NTN and terrestrial approaches.

</details>


### [126] [A Unified Benchmark for RCM-Constrained Visual Servoing: Modeling-Controller Interaction and Robustness Analysis in Laparoscopic Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00030)
*Jing Zhang,Mengtang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个开源仿真框架，统一集成了三种RCM建模方法和六种IBVS控制架构，用于系统评估机器人辅助腹腔镜微创手术中RCM约束下的视觉伺服控制，揭示了建模与控制交互中的关键结构敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助腹腔镜微创手术中，RCM约束的精确执行对于安全稳定的自动视野调整至关重要。尽管基于控制的RCM策略被广泛采用，但由于缺乏统一且可复现的基准，不同RCM公式和IBVS框架的系统性比较仍然困难。

Method: 开发了一个开源仿真框架，在统一的速度级公式下集成了三种代表性RCM建模方法和六种基于图像的视觉伺服（IBVS）控制架构，通过结构化案例研究进行受控且一致的评估。

Result: 框架揭示了建模与控制器交互产生的关键结构敏感性，包括切平面定义的影响、约束维度、开环与闭环执行方式、以及运动学奇点附近的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架为RCM约束下的视觉伺服研究提供了可复现的基础，所有资源已开源，并附有补充视频演示。

Abstract: In robot-assisted laparoscopic minimally invasive surgery (MIS), accurate enforcement of the remote center of motion (RCM) constraint is critical for safe and stable automatic field-of-view (FoV) adjustment. Although control-based RCM strategies are widely adopted due to their flexibility and cost-effectiveness, systematic comparison of different RCM formulations and image-based visual servoing (IBVS) frameworks remains challenging due to the lack of a unified and reproducible benchmark. This paper presents an open-source simulation framework integrating three representative RCM modeling approaches and six IBVS-based control architectures within a unified velocity-level formulation, enabling controlled and consistent evaluation. Through structured case studies, the framework reveals key structural sensitivities arising from modeling and controller interactions, including the impact of tangent-plane definition, constraint dimensionality, open- versus closed-loop enforcement, and robustness near kinematic singularities. All resources are released and demostrations are provided in the supplementary video, providing a reproducible foundation for RCM-constrained visual servoing research.

</details>


### [127] [Joint Discovery of Object and Action Symbols through Effect Prediction for Robotic Manipulation Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00031)
*Burcu Kilic,Berke Kartal,Fatih Dogangun,Erhan Oztop,Emre Ugur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种通过二值瓶颈层联合发现操作基元与物体类别的模型，基于交互效果进行规划，在桌面物体重定位与堆叠任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么基于视觉外观分类物体（无法区分外观相似但行为不同的物体），要么基于交互效果但局限于预定义动作，未能同时学习操作基元和物体类别。

Method: 通过二值瓶颈层联合发现高层操作基元和物体类别，模型训练以预测多模态交互结果（物体运动、接触、力反馈）；利用离散规划方法结合中间步骤实现部分动作执行；通过少量交互效果比较实现少样本泛化。

Result: 在桌面物体重定位和堆叠任务中，该方法在规划精度上优于最先进方法和基于视觉的对比方法，且在未见物体上表现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 基于交互效果的离散符号表示结合二值瓶颈的联合学习方法，能够有效实现复杂操作规划，且基于行为的物体类别分配比视觉外观具有更好的泛化能力。

Abstract: To perform complex manipulation planning, autonomous robots are required to abstract continuous, high-dimensional sensorimotor interactions into discrete object and action representations. Earlier work either categorized objects based on visual appearances, which fails to distinguish objects that appear similar but behave differently, or based on effects under interaction, but was limited to predefined actions. To address these limitations, we propose a model that jointly discovers high-level manipulation primitives and object categories through a binary bottleneck layer, trained to predict multi-modal outcomes, including object motion, contact, and force feedback, from random interaction data. Building on these discovered binary representations, we leverage a discrete planning method that uses intermediate steps in the predicted effect trajectory to enable partial action executions for precise low-level control. Additionally, we evaluate our framework's generalization capabilities on novel objects by assigning object categories through comparing a small number of interaction effects with the predicted effects of learned object symbols, enabling few-shot generalization based on behavior rather than visual similarity. We conduct experiments on tabletop repositioning and stacking tasks, and confirm that our effect-driven planning approach outperforms both a state-of-the-art method and a visual-based alternative in planning precision across seen and novel objects.

</details>


### [128] [Learning Dexterous Manipulation Using Contact Wrench Guidance From Human Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00033)
*Xinghao Zhu,Zixi Liu,Shalin Jain,Chenran Li,Milad Noori,Huihua Zhao,John Welsh,Michael Andres Lin,Wei Liu,Tingwu Wang,Xingye Da,Zhengyi Luo,Vishal Kulkarni,Naema Bhatti,Yuke Zhu,Linxi Fan,Bowen Wen,Danfei Xu,Soha Pouya,Yan Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CHORD是一个利用人类演示通过物体中心接触力/力矩空间引导来训练灵巧操作机器人策略的框架，在包含4,739个双手灵巧操作任务的大规模仿真基准上平均成功率达82.12%，并成功迁移到真实世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示数据丰富但难以直接迁移到机器人策略，现有方法在处理接触丰富的灵巧操作任务时可扩展性不足。

Method: 提出物体中心接触力/力矩空间引导（CHORD），将人类和机器人的运动表示为它们能在物体上诱导的力和力矩，通过诱导的瞬时运动测量相似性，利用该引导增强强化学习的可扩展性。

Result: 在1,831个基准任务上平均成功率82.12%；从仅手部/第三人称演示泛化到全身操作，成功率达90.77%；策略在开环和闭环设置下均迁移到真实世界。

Conclusion: 基于物体中心接触力/力矩空间引导能够有效弥合人类演示与机器人策略之间的鸿沟，为长时域、接触丰富的灵巧操作提供可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Dexterous robot manipulation can benefit from the abundance of human demonstrations, but transferring such demonstrations to robot policies remains challenging. We present Contact Wrench Guidance from Human Demonstration in Robotic Dexterous Manipulation (CHORD), a framework for long-horizon manipulation of rigid and articulated objects with reinforcement learning. The key idea is object-centric contact wrench space guidance: we represent human and robot motions by the forces and torques they can induce on the object, enabling similarity to be measured by the induced instantaneous motions. This guidance makes reinforcement learning more scalable for contact-rich dexterous manipulation. We further introduce a large-scale simulation benchmark with 4,739 bimanual dexterous manipulation tasks, constructed from motion-capture datasets and reconstructed in-house videos. Evaluated on 1,831 benchmark tasks, CHORD achieves an average success rate of 82.12%, demonstrating strong scalability. CHORD also generalizes to whole-body manipulation from hand-only and third-person demonstrations, achieving a 90.77% success rate, and the learned policies transfer to the real world in both open-loop and closed-loop settings.

</details>


### [129] [Optimal any-angle path planning in static and dynamic environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00065)
*Yiyuan Zou,Clark Borst*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Zeta*和Zeta*-SIPP两种最优任意角度路径规划算法，通过椭圆前向扩展和视场技术加速计算，在静态和动态环境中均保持最优性。Zeta*-SIPP比当前最优规划器TO-AA-SIPP快20倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任意角度路径规划算法大多无法在动态障碍物存在下保证最优解，亟需一种既能保持最优性又能高效计算的统一方法。

Method: 提出两种通用加速技术：1)椭圆前向扩展，利用椭圆邻域限制搜索空间；2)视场技术，替代传统视线方法加速可见性检查。引入反向扫描和前向扫描以整合上述技术，并据此构建Zeta*（静态）和Zeta*-SIPP（动态）算法。

Result: Zeta*与当前最优算法Anya性能相当；Zeta*-SIPP无论采用哪种扫描方式，均比TO-AA-SIPP快20倍以上。

Conclusion: 该研究明确了实现最优任意角度路径规划的关键需求，提出了一种适用于不同环境的统一方法，且Zeta*可便捷扩展至动态环境等更广泛场景。

Abstract: Any-angle path planning extends traditional graph-based path planning by allowing movement between any pair of vertices, rather than being restricted by predefined edges. It can find straighter and shorter paths in continuous space with graphs, making it particularly suitable for navigation in open areas such as airspaces, warehouses, and oceans. Many any-angle path-planning algorithms have been proposed, but only a few can guarantee optimal solutions, especially in the presence of dynamic obstacles. To address this challenge, this article focuses on optimal any-angle path planning on grids and introduces two general techniques that accelerate computation while preserving optimality in both static and dynamic environments: 1) elliptical forward expansion, which leverages ellipse-based neighborhoods to restrict the search space, and 2) field of view, which replaces traditional line-of-sight methods to speed up visibility checks. To integrate these two techniques, inverted and forward scanning are introduced. Inverted scanning establishes visual connections from open nodes, whereas forward scanning initiates scans from closed nodes. Building on the proposed techniques, Zeta* and Zeta*-SIPP are developed for static and dynamic environments respectively. Zeta*, when combined with forward scanning, is similar to the state-of-the-art algorithm Anya and attains comparable performance. Unlike Anya, Zeta* can be readily extended to other settings, such as dynamic environments (e.g., Zeta*-SIPP). Zeta*-SIPP, with either scanning method, is more than 20 times faster than the corresponding state-of-the-art optimal planner TO-AA-SIPP. Overall, this research identifies the key requirements for achieving optimal any-angle path planning and introduces a unified approach suitable for different environments.

</details>


### [130] [Learning Expert Strategy for Autonomous Robotic Endovascular Intervention via Decoupled Procedural Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00066)
*Yanxi Chen,Tianliang Yao,Shaolong Tang,Jiyuan Zhao,Hengyu Hu,Zhaoxing Li,Antonio J. Sánchez Egea,Peng Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种将强化学习战略决策与专家经验执行相结合的双层框架，用于自主血管内介入导航，在仿真和真实机器人平台上实现了>96%的成功率、29.3%的操作步骤减少和13%的轨迹方差降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血管内介入手术对设备操作精度要求极高，手动操作存在性能差异，而现有强化学习方法在约束满足和安全保证方面表现不足。

Method: 将高层次战略决策与低层次程序执行显式解耦：强化学习策略生成全局导航意图，专家知情执行模块确保机器人运动严格遵循专家操作规范、实时运动学限制和血管安全约束。

Result: 导航成功率>96%，操作步骤减少29.3%，轨迹方差降低13%，在3D仿真和真实机器人平台上均优于基线策略。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效提升自主血管内介入手术的可预测性、安全性和一致性，使自主行为与临床规范对齐。

Abstract: Endovascular interventions are high-stakes procedures requiring precise device operation within complex and tortuous vascular anatomies. Autonomous endovascular navigation has the potential to standardize procedural quality and reduce the performance variability inherent in manual operation. Although Reinforcement Learning (RL) approaches have demonstrated promise in enabling autonomy in endovascular intervention, they often struggle with explicit constraint satisfaction and safety guarantees. To address these challenges, a learning-based expert strategy is introduced, enhancing procedural consistency in autonomous endovascular intervention by explicitly decoupling high-level strategic decision-making from low-level procedural execution. The proposed framework replicates the expert clinical decision-making process: a strategic RL policy generates global navigation intents, which are subsequently refined through an expert-informed execution module. This module ensures that robot movements strictly adhere to expert operational norms, real-time kinematic limits, and vessel safety constraints.
